**本章主线：** 选择餐食 → 餐具饮品 → 采购食材 → 调味备料 → 烹饪进食 → 饮食体验

**最后尝试回答：** 从点餐或购物出发，说明一顿饭的食材、准备、烹饪和味道。

### 词群 12.01｜从饮食需求到点餐 {#group-12-01}

**串联方式：** 先说饮食、胃口与可食用性，再用菜谱、菜系、菜单和外带组织点餐；认识厨师、美食家与素食者。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="12-001">
<summary><h4 id="word-12-001" class="vocabulary-word-title">order<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-12-001" aria-label="定位 order">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="12-001" data-mark-label="order" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="order：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="order：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=order&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 order" title="在有道查询 order">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">12-001</span></span></summary>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="12-003">
<summary><h4 id="word-12-003" class="vocabulary-word-title">diet<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-12-003" aria-label="定位 diet">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="12-003" data-mark-label="diet" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="diet：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="diet：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=diet&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 diet" title="在有道查询 diet">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">12-003</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.日常饮食；(医生指定的)特别饮食；节食</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">With her appetite restored, she added a wider range of edible vegetables to her <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=diet&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>diet</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">食欲恢复后，她在饮食中加入了种类更多的可食用蔬菜。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">His travel diary recorded his <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=diet&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>diet</strong></a>, appetite and the edible plants available during the expedition.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">他的旅行日记记录了探险期间的饮食、食欲以及能够找到的可食用植物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The chef’s colourful <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=diet&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>diet</strong></a> of edible flowers and vegetables appealed to the guest’s appetite.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">厨师提供的由食用花和蔬菜组成的多彩饮食引起了客人的食欲。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a balanced diet</span> · 均衡饮食</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=nutrition&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">nutrition</a></span> · 营养</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=poisonous&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">poisonous</a></span> · 有毒的</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">His travel diary recorded his diet, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=appetite&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>appetite</strong></a> and the edible plants available during the expedition.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">他的旅行日记记录了探险期间的饮食、食欲以及能够找到的可食用植物。</p></li>

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.请(客)对待治疗</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The charity will cater for older residents and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=treat&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>treat</strong></a> them to lunch as part of its food provision.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">该慈善机构将为老年居民提供餐饮，并把请他们吃午餐作为食品供应服务的一部分。</p></li>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Better food provision allows the centre to cater for more families and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=treat&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>treat</strong></a> children to a hot meal.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">改善食品供应让中心能为更多家庭提供餐饮，并请孩子们吃上一顿热饭。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">treat someone to dinner</span> · 请某人吃晚餐</p>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The charity will cater for older residents and treat them to lunch as part of its food <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=provision&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>provision</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">该慈善机构将为老年居民提供餐饮，并把请他们吃午餐作为食品供应服务的一部分。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We can cater for the visitors and treat them to local dishes if the budget for food <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=provision&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>provision</strong></a> increases.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果食品供应预算增加，我们就能为访客提供餐饮，请他们品尝当地菜。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Better food <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=provision&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>provision</strong></a> allows the centre to cater for more families and treat children to a hot meal.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">改善食品供应让中心能为更多家庭提供餐饮，并请孩子们吃上一顿热饭。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">food provision</span> · 食品供应</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hospitality&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">hospitality</a></span> · 款待</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=catering&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">catering</a></span> · 餐饮服务</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="12-008">
<summary><h4 id="word-12-008" class="vocabulary-word-title">edible<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-12-008" aria-label="定位 edible">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="12-008" data-mark-label="edible" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="edible：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="edible：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=edible&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 edible" title="在有道查询 edible">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">12-008</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj.可以吃的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">With her appetite restored, she added a wider range of <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=edible&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>edible</strong></a> vegetables to her diet.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">食欲恢复后，她在饮食中加入了种类更多的可食用蔬菜。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">His travel diary recorded his diet, appetite and the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=edible&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>edible</strong></a> plants available during the expedition.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">他的旅行日记记录了探险期间的饮食、食欲以及能够找到的可食用植物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The chef’s colourful diet of <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=edible&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>edible</strong></a> flowers and vegetables appealed to the guest’s appetite.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">厨师提供的由食用花和蔬菜组成的多彩饮食引起了客人的食欲。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">edible plants</span> · 可食用植物</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=nutrition&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">nutrition</a></span> · 营养</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=poisonous&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">poisonous</a></span> · 有毒的</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="12-009">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.食谱；秘诀</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The chef adapted a traditional <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=recipe&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>recipe</strong></a> to introduce visitors to local cuisine.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">厨师改编了一道传统食谱，向游客介绍当地菜肴。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A good chef can explain how a simple <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=recipe&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>recipe</strong></a> reflects a region’s cuisine.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">好厨师能解释一道简单食谱如何体现某地的烹饪特色。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The chef shared a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=recipe&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>recipe</strong></a> that combines French technique with Chinese cuisine.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">厨师分享了一道结合法式技法与中式烹饪的食谱。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">follow a recipe</span> · 照食谱做</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cookbook&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">cookbook</a></span> · 食谱书</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=culinary%20tradition&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">culinary tradition</a></span> · 烹饪传统</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The new <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=takeaway&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>takeaway</strong></a> serves gourmet vegetarian meals.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">新开的外卖店供应精致素食。</p></li>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">

### 词群 12.02｜在哪里吃，吃哪一餐 {#group-12-02}

**串联方式：** 比较餐馆、食堂、酒吧和咖啡厅，再选自助、烧烤、晚餐、宴会及小食。

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The hotel has a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=restaurant&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>restaurant</strong></a> for supper and a small bar for drinks.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">酒店有供应晚餐的餐馆和一家供人喝酒的小酒吧。</p></li>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The college replaced its <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=refectory&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>refectory</strong></a> buffet with a modern cafeteria service.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">学院把食堂的自助餐改成了现代化的自选餐饮服务。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Unlike the cafeteria, the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=refectory&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>refectory</strong></a> offers a fixed-price buffet.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">与自选餐厅不同，这家食堂供应固定价格的自助餐。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Students can choose the buffet in the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=refectory&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>refectory</strong></a> or buy a sandwich in the cafeteria.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">学生可以选择食堂里的自助餐，也可以在自选餐厅买三明治。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="12-015">
<summary><h4 id="word-12-015" class="vocabulary-word-title">barbecue<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-12-015" aria-label="定位 barbecue">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="12-015" data-mark-label="barbecue" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="barbecue：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="barbecue：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=barbecue&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 barbecue" title="在有道查询 barbecue">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">12-015</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.烧烤野餐；烤架；v.烧烤</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We chose a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=barbecue&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>barbecue</strong></a> instead of a formal banquet for the reunion.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们选择用烧烤聚会代替正式宴会来庆祝团聚。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The hotel can arrange either a beach <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=barbecue&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>barbecue</strong></a> or an indoor banquet.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">酒店可以安排沙滩烧烤或室内宴会。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Rain forced us to move the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=barbecue&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>barbecue</strong></a> indoors and turn it into a banquet.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">雨迫使我们把烧烤移到室内，改办宴会。</p></li>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The hotel can arrange either a beach barbecue or an indoor <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=banquet&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>banquet</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">酒店可以安排沙滩烧烤或室内宴会。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Rain forced us to move the barbecue indoors and turn it into a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=banquet&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>banquet</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">雨迫使我们把烧烤移到室内，改办宴会。</p></li>
</ol>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The camping kit includes a folding <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=knife&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>knife</strong></a>, a fork and a spoon.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">露营套装包括一把折叠刀、一把叉子和一把勺子。</p></li>
</ol>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The tray held a mug, a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=glass&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>glass</strong></a> and a small saucer.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">托盘上放着一个大杯、一个玻璃杯和一个小茶托。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Put the mug on the saucer and carry the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=glass&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>glass</strong></a> on the tray.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">把大杯放在茶托上，用托盘端玻璃杯。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=glass&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>glass</strong></a> slipped from the tray and struck the mug and saucer.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一个玻璃杯从托盘滑落，撞到了大杯和茶托。</p></li>
</ol>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="12-040">
<summary><h4 id="word-12-040" class="vocabulary-word-title">kettle<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-12-040" aria-label="定位 kettle">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="12-040" data-mark-label="kettle" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="kettle：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="kettle：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=kettle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 kettle" title="在有道查询 kettle">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">12-040</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.水壶【美】锅</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=kettle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>kettle</strong></a> and a soup pot stood on the stove.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">炉子上放着一把水壶和一口汤锅。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Move the pot to the back of the stove before heating the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=kettle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>kettle</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">烧水壶前先把锅移到炉子后方。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The old stove could heat a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=kettle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>kettle</strong></a> and a large pot at the same time.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">旧炉子可以同时烧一壶水和一大锅东西。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">boil a kettle</span> · 烧一壶水</p>

</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="12-041">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Put the cake tin in the oven and cover the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pan&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pan</strong></a> with a lid.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">把蛋糕模具放进烤箱，并给平底锅盖上盖子。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Use the oven for the bread tin while the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pan&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pan</strong></a> heats under its lid.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">用烤箱烤面包模具里的面包，同时让盖着盖子的平底锅加热。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pan&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pan</strong></a> has an oven-safe lid, but the cake tin has none.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">平底锅有一个可放进烤箱的盖子，但蛋糕模具没有盖子。</p></li>
</ol>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Move the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pot&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pot</strong></a> to the back of the stove before heating the kettle.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">烧水壶前先把锅移到炉子后方。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The old stove could heat a kettle and a large <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pot&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pot</strong></a> at the same time.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">旧炉子可以同时烧一壶水和一大锅东西。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a cooking pot</span> · 煮锅</p>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Move the pot to the back of the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stove&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>stove</strong></a> before heating the kettle.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">烧水壶前先把锅移到炉子后方。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The old <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stove&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>stove</strong></a> could heat a kettle and a large pot at the same time.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">旧炉子可以同时烧一壶水和一大锅东西。</p></li>

### 词群 12.04｜饮品、酒精与烟草 {#group-12-04}

<summary><h4 id="word-12-051" class="vocabulary-word-title">soda<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-12-051" aria-label="定位 soda">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="12-051" data-mark-label="soda" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="soda：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="soda：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=soda&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 soda" title="在有道查询 soda">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">12-051</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.无水碳酸钠；苏打水；【美】汽水</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">For a cold beverage, you can drink juice or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=soda&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>soda</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">想喝冷饮的话，你可以喝果汁或汽水。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">I prefer to drink juice, but the café’s cheapest beverage is <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=soda&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>soda</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我更喜欢喝果汁，但这家咖啡馆最便宜的饮料是汽水。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Choose juice instead of <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=soda&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>soda</strong></a> if you want a beverage to drink with breakfast.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">早餐想喝饮料的话，可以选择果汁而非汽水。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a can of soda</span> · 一罐汽水</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mineral%20water&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">mineral water</a></span> · 矿泉水</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=soft%20drink&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">soft drink</a></span> · 不含酒精的饮料</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="12-052">
<summary><h4 id="word-12-052" class="vocabulary-word-title">coffee<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-12-052" aria-label="定位 coffee">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="12-052" data-mark-label="coffee" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="coffee：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="coffee：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=coffee&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 coffee" title="在有道查询 coffee">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">12-052</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.咖啡</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The café serves <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=coffee&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>coffee</strong></a> but needs a licence to sell alcohol, including liquor.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">咖啡馆供应咖啡，但售卖包括烈酒在内的酒精饮品需要许可证。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">He ordered <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=coffee&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>coffee</strong></a> because he avoids alcohol and never drinks liquor.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">他点了咖啡，因为他不喝酒，也从不喝烈酒。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The menu lists <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=coffee&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>coffee</strong></a> separately from alcohol, with liquor in its own section.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">菜单将咖啡与酒精饮品分开列出，烈酒另列一栏。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">black coffee</span> · 黑咖啡</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=caffeine&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">caffeine</a></span> · 咖啡因</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=spirits&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">spirits</a></span> · 烈酒</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="12-055">
<summary><h4 id="word-12-055" class="vocabulary-word-title">whisky<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-12-055" aria-label="定位 whisky">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="12-055" data-mark-label="whisky" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="whisky：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="whisky：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=whisky&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 whisky" title="在有道查询 whisky">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">12-055</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">(=whiskey)；n.威士忌酒</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">He stayed sober while his guests became drunk on <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=whisky&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>whisky</strong></a> and brandy.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">客人喝威士忌和白兰地喝醉了，他却保持清醒。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A sober driver refused the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=whisky&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>whisky</strong></a> and brandy offered by a drunk friend.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一位清醒的司机拒绝了醉酒朋友递来的威士忌和白兰地。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Whether you choose <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=whisky&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>whisky</strong></a> or brandy, arrange a sober driver before anyone gets drunk.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">无论选择威士忌还是白兰地，都要在有人喝醉前安排好清醒的司机。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a glass of whisky</span> · 一杯威士忌</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=intoxication&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">intoxication</a></span> · 醉酒</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=designated%20driver&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">designated driver</a></span> · 指定的不饮酒司机</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="12-056">
<summary><h4 id="word-12-056" class="vocabulary-word-title">brandy<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-12-056" aria-label="定位 brandy">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="12-056" data-mark-label="brandy" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="brandy：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="brandy：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=brandy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 brandy" title="在有道查询 brandy">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">12-056</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.白兰地酒</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">He stayed sober while his guests became drunk on whisky and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=brandy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>brandy</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">客人喝威士忌和白兰地喝醉了，他却保持清醒。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A sober driver refused the whisky and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=brandy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>brandy</strong></a> offered by a drunk friend.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一位清醒的司机拒绝了醉酒朋友递来的威士忌和白兰地。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Whether you choose whisky or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=brandy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>brandy</strong></a>, arrange a sober driver before anyone gets drunk.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">无论选择威士忌还是白兰地，都要在有人喝醉前安排好清醒的司机。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a bottle of brandy</span> · 一瓶白兰地</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=intoxication&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">intoxication</a></span> · 醉酒</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=designated%20driver&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">designated driver</a></span> · 指定的不饮酒司机</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="12-057">
<summary><h4 id="word-12-057" class="vocabulary-word-title">drunk<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-12-057" aria-label="定位 drunk">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="12-057" data-mark-label="drunk" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="drunk：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="drunk：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=drunk&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 drunk" title="在有道查询 drunk">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">12-057</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj.醉的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">He stayed sober while his guests became <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=drunk&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>drunk</strong></a> on whisky and brandy.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">客人喝威士忌和白兰地喝醉了，他却保持清醒。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A sober driver refused the whisky and brandy offered by a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=drunk&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>drunk</strong></a> friend.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一位清醒的司机拒绝了醉酒朋友递来的威士忌和白兰地。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Whether you choose whisky or brandy, arrange a sober driver before anyone gets <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=drunk&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>drunk</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">无论选择威士忌还是白兰地，都要在有人喝醉前安排好清醒的司机。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">get drunk</span> · 喝醉</p>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="12-059">
<summary><h4 id="word-12-059" class="vocabulary-word-title">tobacco<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-12-059" aria-label="定位 tobacco">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="12-059" data-mark-label="tobacco" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="tobacco：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="tobacco：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tobacco&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 tobacco" title="在有道查询 tobacco">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">12-059</span></span></summary>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A cigarette contains <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tobacco&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tobacco</strong></a> that releases harmful chemicals when burned.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">香烟含有烟草，燃烧时会释放有害化学物质。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The tax increase affected every cigarette and other <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tobacco&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tobacco</strong></a> product.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">增税影响了每一支香烟及其他烟草产品。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The campaign showed how cigarette advertising can encourage <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tobacco&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tobacco</strong></a> use.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这场宣传展示了香烟广告如何促进烟草使用。</p></li>
</ol>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="12-061">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj.清醒的头脑清醒的；v.(使)醒酒</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">He stayed <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sober&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sober</strong></a> while his guests became drunk on whisky and brandy.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">客人喝威士忌和白兰地喝醉了，他却保持清醒。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sober&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sober</strong></a> driver refused the whisky and brandy offered by a drunk friend.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一位清醒的司机拒绝了醉酒朋友递来的威士忌和白兰地。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Whether you choose whisky or brandy, arrange a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sober&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sober</strong></a> driver before anyone gets drunk.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">无论选择威士忌还是白兰地，都要在有人喝醉前安排好清醒的司机。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">stay sober</span> · 保持清醒</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=intoxication&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">intoxication</a></span> · 醉酒</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=designated%20driver&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">designated driver</a></span> · 指定的不饮酒司机</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The vegetarian burger uses <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mushroom&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mushroom</strong></a> and eggplant for a rich texture.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这款素汉堡用蘑菇和茄子营造丰富口感。</p></li>

</ol>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=carrot&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>carrot</strong></a> tastes sweeter than the turnip and the peppery radish.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">胡萝卜比芜菁和带辛辣味的萝卜更甜。</p></li>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Slice a carrot, a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=turnip&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>turnip</strong></a> and a radish for the salad.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">把一根胡萝卜、一个芜菁和一个萝卜切片做沙拉。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The carrot tastes sweeter than the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=turnip&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>turnip</strong></a> and the peppery radish.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">胡萝卜比芜菁和带辛辣味的萝卜更甜。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We pulled a carrot, a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=turnip&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>turnip</strong></a> and a radish from the garden.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们从菜园里拔了一根胡萝卜、一个芜菁和一个萝卜。</p></li>

### 词群 12.06｜水果与去皮去核 {#group-12-06}

**串联方式：** 先认水果，再把剥皮、剥除、果核和外壳等处理动作连到水果上。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="12-058">
<summary><h4 id="word-12-058" class="vocabulary-word-title">peel<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-12-058" aria-label="定位 peel">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="12-058" data-mark-label="peel" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="peel：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="peel：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=peel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 peel" title="在有道查询 peel">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">12-058</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">V. 剥落；掉漆；脱皮</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">For the fruit salad, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=peel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>peel</strong></a> the apple and remove its core, strip the orange of its rind, and hull the strawberries.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">做水果沙拉时，给苹果削皮去核，剥掉橙皮，并去掉草莓蒂。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before mixing the fruit, hull the strawberries, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=peel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>peel</strong></a> and core the pear, and strip away any tough skin.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">混合水果前，去掉草莓蒂，给梨削皮去核，并剥掉所有坚硬果皮。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The fruit preparation guide explains how to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=peel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>peel</strong></a> and core apples, strip citrus rind, and hull strawberries.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">水果处理指南介绍了如何给苹果削皮去核、剥除柑橘果皮和去掉草莓蒂。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">peel an apple</span> · 给苹果削皮</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rind&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">rind</a></span> · 厚果皮</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pith&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">pith</a></span> · 果皮内的白瓤</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="12-075">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">For the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fruit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fruit</strong></a> salad, peel the apple and remove its core, strip the orange of its rind, and hull the strawberries.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">做水果沙拉时，给苹果削皮去核，剥掉橙皮，并去掉草莓蒂。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before mixing the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fruit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fruit</strong></a>, hull the strawberries, peel and core the pear, and strip away any tough skin.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">混合水果前，去掉草莓蒂，给梨削皮去核，并剥掉所有坚硬果皮。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fruit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fruit</strong></a> preparation guide explains how to peel and core apples, strip citrus rind, and hull strawberries.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">水果处理指南介绍了如何给苹果削皮去核、剥除柑橘果皮和去掉草莓蒂。</p></li>
</ol>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The tropical fruit bowl contains papaya, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=melon&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>melon</strong></a> and kiwi.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这碗热带风味水果里有木瓜、甜瓜和猕猴桃。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Blend papaya and kiwi with a little <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=melon&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>melon</strong></a> for a refreshing drink.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">把木瓜、猕猴桃和少量甜瓜搅打成清爽的饮料。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We served chilled <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=melon&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>melon</strong></a> with papaya and kiwi for dessert.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们把冰镇甜瓜与木瓜、猕猴桃一起作为甜点端上。</p></li>
</ol>

### 词群 12.07｜谷物、畜肉与鱼类 {#group-12-07}

**串联方式：** 从庄稼、收割、谷物和面粉进入粥与糊，再识别畜禽肉类及捕鱼工具。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="12-090">
<summary><h4 id="word-12-090" class="vocabulary-word-title">crop<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-12-090" aria-label="定位 crop">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="12-090" data-mark-label="crop" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="crop：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="crop：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=crop&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 crop" title="在有道查询 crop">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">12-090</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.庄稼；收成，产量</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Farmers reap the wheat <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=crop&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>crop</strong></a> before storing the grain beside the corn.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">农民收割小麦后，把谷物存放在玉米旁边。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To reap a good <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=crop&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>crop</strong></a>, growers must protect both wheat and corn from grain pests.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">要取得好收成，种植者必须保护小麦和玉米免受谷物害虫侵害。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The cooperative will reap its wheat <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=crop&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>crop</strong></a> this week and dry the grain while the corn continues to grow.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">合作社本周将收割小麦并晾晒谷物，此时玉米仍在生长。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a cereal crop</span> · 谷类作物</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=harvest&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">harvest</a></span> · 收割</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=silo&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">silo</a></span> · 粮仓</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="12-091">
<summary><h4 id="word-12-091" class="vocabulary-word-title">corn<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-12-091" aria-label="定位 corn">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="12-091" data-mark-label="corn" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="corn：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="corn：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=corn&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 corn" title="在有道查询 corn">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">12-091</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.【英】(小麦等)谷物【美】玉米</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Farmers reap the wheat crop before storing the grain beside the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=corn&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>corn</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">农民收割小麦后，把谷物存放在玉米旁边。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To reap a good crop, growers must protect both wheat and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=corn&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>corn</strong></a> from grain pests.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">要取得好收成，种植者必须保护小麦和玉米免受谷物害虫侵害。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The cooperative will reap its wheat crop this week and dry the grain while the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=corn&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>corn</strong></a> continues to grow.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">合作社本周将收割小麦并晾晒谷物，此时玉米仍在生长。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">sweet corn</span> · 甜玉米</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=harvest&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">harvest</a></span> · 收割</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=silo&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">silo</a></span> · 粮仓</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<summary><h4 id="word-12-094" class="vocabulary-word-title">reap<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-12-094" aria-label="定位 reap">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="12-094" data-mark-label="reap" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="reap：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="reap：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reap&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 reap" title="在有道查询 reap">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">12-094</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.收割；收获</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Farmers <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reap&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reap</strong></a> the wheat crop before storing the grain beside the corn.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">农民收割小麦后，把谷物存放在玉米旁边。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reap&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reap</strong></a> a good crop, growers must protect both wheat and corn from grain pests.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">要取得好收成，种植者必须保护小麦和玉米免受谷物害虫侵害。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The cooperative will <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reap&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reap</strong></a> its wheat crop this week and dry the grain while the corn continues to grow.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">合作社本周将收割小麦并晾晒谷物，此时玉米仍在生长。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">reap a harvest</span> · 收获庄稼</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=harvest&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">harvest</a></span> · 收割</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=silo&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">silo</a></span> · 粮仓</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Mix the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=flour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>flour</strong></a> into a paste before stirring it into the porridge.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">把面粉调成糊，再搅入粥中。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Too much <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=flour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>flour</strong></a> can turn smooth porridge into a thick paste.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">面粉太多会把顺滑的粥变成稠糊。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The recipe uses rice <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=flour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>flour</strong></a> to make a paste that thickens the porridge.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">食谱用米粉调糊来使粥变稠。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">plain flour</span> · 普通面粉</p>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The farm keeps <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=livestock&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>livestock</strong></a> in one field and raises chicken and turkey breeds in another.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">农场在一块地饲养家畜，在另一块地饲养不同品种的鸡和火鸡。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=livestock&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>livestock</strong></a> inspector checked every chicken and turkey enclosure.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">畜牧检查员检查了每一处鸡舍和火鸡围栏。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=livestock&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Livestock</strong></a> feed prices have increased the cost of raising both chicken and turkey flocks.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">畜禽饲料涨价提高了饲养鸡群和火鸡群的成本。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">raise livestock</span> · 饲养家畜</p>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The livestock inspector checked every <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=chicken&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>chicken</strong></a> and turkey enclosure.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">畜牧检查员检查了每一处鸡舍和火鸡围栏。</p></li>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The butcher sells <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=beef&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>beef</strong></a>, pork and mutton, as well as handmade sausage.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">肉贩出售牛肉、猪肉、羊肉和手工香肠。</p></li>

### 词群 12.08｜乳制品与营养补充 {#group-12-08}

**串联方式：** 把奶、酸奶、奶油、奶酪和黄油放在乳制品柜；supplement 是补充物的延伸。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="12-108">
<summary><h4 id="word-12-108" class="vocabulary-word-title">dairy<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-12-108" aria-label="定位 dairy">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="12-108" data-mark-label="dairy" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="dairy：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="dairy：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dairy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 dairy" title="在有道查询 dairy">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">12-108</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.乳制品；乳品店；adj.乳制的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dairy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>dairy</strong></a> counter stocks milk, yogurt and cream.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">乳品柜台备有牛奶、酸奶和奶油。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Use milk or yogurt instead of cream for a lighter <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dairy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>dairy</strong></a> dessert.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">用牛奶或酸奶代替奶油，可以做出更清淡的乳制甜点。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">This <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dairy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>dairy</strong></a>-free recipe replaces milk, yogurt and cream with plant-based alternatives.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这道不含乳制品的食谱用植物替代品代替牛奶、酸奶和奶油。</p></li>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Her shopping list included <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cheese&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cheese</strong></a>, butter and a vitamin supplement.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她的购物单上有奶酪、黄油和维生素补充剂。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The meal plan uses a little <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cheese&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cheese</strong></a> and butter, with a supplement only if advised.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这份饮食计划使用少量奶酪和黄油，只在获得建议时使用补充剂。</p></li>

</ol>

### 词群 12.09｜主食、简餐与甜点 {#group-12-09}

**串联方式：** 从沙拉、三明治和面包到意面与汤，再选布丁、饼干、果酱、巧克力和冰淇淋。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="12-115">
<summary><h4 id="word-12-115" class="vocabulary-word-title">salad<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-12-115" aria-label="定位 salad">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="12-115" data-mark-label="salad" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="salad：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="salad：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=salad&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 salad" title="在有道查询 salad">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">12-115</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.色拉</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">She chose a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=salad&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>salad</strong></a> while I ordered a sandwich and he had a hamburger.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她选了沙拉，我点了三明治，他吃了汉堡。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The lunch deal includes a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=salad&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>salad</strong></a> with either a sandwich or a hamburger.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">午餐套餐包括一份沙拉，以及三明治或汉堡二选一。</p></li>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The bakery sold its last <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=loaf&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>loaf</strong></a> just as the pie and pizza came out of the oven.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">派和比萨刚出炉时，面包店卖掉了最后一条面包。</p></li>

</ol>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We broke the spaghetti into short pieces to use as <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pasta&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pasta</strong></a> in the soup.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们把意大利细面条折成小段，作为面食加进汤里。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pasta&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pasta</strong></a> course was spaghetti, followed by a bowl of soup.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">面食菜是意大利细面条，随后上了一碗汤。</p></li>

</ol>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We broke the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=spaghetti&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>spaghetti</strong></a> into short pieces to use as pasta in the soup.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们把意大利细面条折成小段，作为面食加进汤里。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The pasta course was <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=spaghetti&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>spaghetti</strong></a>, followed by a bowl of soup.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">面食菜是意大利细面条，随后上了一碗汤。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="12-127">

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.坚果；螺母</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The vanilla ice cream has a chocolate swirl and a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=nut&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>nut</strong></a> topping.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">香草冰淇淋里有巧克力纹路，上面撒着坚果。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">For a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=nut&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>nut</strong></a>-free dessert, serve plain vanilla ice cream with chocolate sauce.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">要做不含坚果的甜点，可以用原味香草冰淇淋配巧克力酱。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Check the chocolate and vanilla ice cream labels if you have a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=nut&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>nut</strong></a> allergy.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果你对坚果过敏，要检查巧克力和香草冰淇淋的标签。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a nut allergy</span> · 坚果过敏</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=topping&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">topping</a></span> · 表层配料</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=allergen&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">allergen</a></span> · 过敏原</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">For a nut-free dessert, serve plain vanilla ice cream with <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=chocolate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>chocolate</strong></a> sauce.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">要做不含坚果的甜点，可以用原味香草冰淇淋配巧克力酱。</p></li>

<summary><h4 id="word-12-130" class="vocabulary-word-title">vanilla<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-12-130" aria-label="定位 vanilla">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="12-130" data-mark-label="vanilla" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="vanilla：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="vanilla：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vanilla&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 vanilla" title="在有道查询 vanilla">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">12-130</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.香草；adj.香草味的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vanilla&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>vanilla</strong></a> ice cream has a chocolate swirl and a nut topping.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">香草冰淇淋里有巧克力纹路，上面撒着坚果。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">For a nut-free dessert, serve plain <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vanilla&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>vanilla</strong></a> ice cream with chocolate sauce.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">要做不含坚果的甜点，可以用原味香草冰淇淋配巧克力酱。</p></li>

</ol>

</div>

### 词群 12.10｜挑选调味料与配料 {#group-12-10}

**串联方式：** 把辛香料、醋盐糖、蜂蜜和酱料放到调味台，再用 ingredient 概括配料。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="12-131">

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Mix <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vinegar&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>vinegar</strong></a>, salt and sugar to make a quick pickle.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">把醋、盐和糖混合，制作快腌菜。</p></li>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The recipe balances the sharp <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vinegar&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>vinegar</strong></a> with a little sugar and salt.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这道食谱用少量糖和盐来平衡醋的酸味。</p></li>
</ol>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Ketchup is the main ingredient in this sauce, with paprika added as a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=spice&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>spice</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">番茄酱是这款酱汁的主要原料，另加红椒粉作为香料。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The sauce needs one more ingredient: a little ketchup to balance the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=spice&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>spice</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这款酱汁还差一种原料：加一点番茄酱来平衡辛香味。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Read the ingredient list on the ketchup before adding it to the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=spice&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>spice</strong></a> sauce.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">把番茄酱加入香料酱汁前，先读它的配料表。</p></li>
</ol>

<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=paprika&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">paprika</a></span> · 红椒粉</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=marinade&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">marinade</a></span> · 腌汁</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="12-156">
<summary><h4 id="word-12-156" class="vocabulary-word-title">ingredient<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-12-156" aria-label="定位 ingredient">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="12-156" data-mark-label="ingredient" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="ingredient：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="ingredient：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ingredient&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 ingredient" title="在有道查询 ingredient">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">12-156</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.成分(烹调的)原料；因素</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Ketchup is the main <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ingredient&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>ingredient</strong></a> in this sauce, with paprika added as a spice.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">番茄酱是这款酱汁的主要原料，另加红椒粉作为香料。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The sauce needs one more <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ingredient&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>ingredient</strong></a>: a little ketchup to balance the spice.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这款酱汁还差一种原料：加一点番茄酱来平衡辛香味。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Read the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ingredient&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>ingredient</strong></a> list on the ketchup before adding it to the spice sauce.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">把番茄酱加入香料酱汁前，先读它的配料表。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a key ingredient</span> · 关键原料</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=paprika&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">paprika</a></span> · 红椒粉</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=marinade&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">marinade</a></span> · 腌汁</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 12.11｜怎样评价味道与香气 {#group-12-11}

**串联方式：** 描述酸甜苦辣与美味；perfume 主要指香水或香气，避免当食物常用调味词。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="12-142">
<summary><h4 id="word-12-142" class="vocabulary-word-title">flavour<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-12-142" aria-label="定位 flavour">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="12-142" data-mark-label="flavour" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="flavour：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="flavour：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=flavour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 flavour" title="在有道查询 flavour">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">12-142</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">(=flavor)；n.味道口味特色，风格</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The sauce has a sweet <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=flavour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>flavour</strong></a> at first, followed by sour and bitter notes.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这款酱汁起初有甜味，接着带出酸味和苦味。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A little sweet honey balances the sour lemon and bitter herbs without hiding their <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=flavour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>flavour</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">少量甜蜂蜜平衡了酸柠檬和苦香草的味道，却没有盖住它们的风味。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Describe the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=flavour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>flavour</strong></a> as sweet, sour or bitter after tasting each sample.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">尝过每份样品后，把味道描述为甜、酸或苦。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a rich flavour</span> · 浓郁的风味</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=aftertaste&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">aftertaste</a></span> · 余味</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=taste%20buds&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">taste buds</a></span> · 味蕾</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="12-143">
<summary><h4 id="word-12-143" class="vocabulary-word-title">sour<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-12-143" aria-label="定位 sour">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="12-143" data-mark-label="sour" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="sour：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="sour：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 sour" title="在有道查询 sour">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">12-143</span></span></summary>
