<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The sauce has a sweet flavour at first, followed by <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sour</strong></a> and bitter notes.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这款酱汁起初有甜味，接着带出酸味和苦味。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A little sweet honey balances the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sour</strong></a> lemon and bitter herbs without hiding their flavour.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">少量甜蜂蜜平衡了酸柠檬和苦香草的味道，却没有盖住它们的风味。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Describe the flavour as sweet, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sour</strong></a> or bitter after tasting each sample.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">尝过每份样品后，把味道描述为甜、酸或苦。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a sour taste</span> · 酸味</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=aftertaste&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">aftertaste</a></span> · 余味</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=taste%20buds&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">taste buds</a></span> · 味蕾</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="12-144">
<summary><h4 id="word-12-144" class="vocabulary-word-title">sweet<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-12-144" aria-label="定位 sweet">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="12-144" data-mark-label="sweet" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="sweet：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="sweet：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sweet&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 sweet" title="在有道查询 sweet">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">12-144</span></span></summary>

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj.甜的；愉快的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A little <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sweet&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sweet</strong></a> honey balances the sour lemon and bitter herbs without hiding their flavour.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">少量甜蜂蜜平衡了酸柠檬和苦香草的味道，却没有盖住它们的风味。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Describe the flavour as <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sweet&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sweet</strong></a>, sour or bitter after tasting each sample.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">尝过每份样品后，把味道描述为甜、酸或苦。</p></li>
</ol>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="12-145">

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The sauce has a sweet flavour at first, followed by sour and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bitter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bitter</strong></a> notes.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这款酱汁起初有甜味，接着带出酸味和苦味。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A little sweet honey balances the sour lemon and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bitter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bitter</strong></a> herbs without hiding their flavour.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">少量甜蜂蜜平衡了酸柠檬和苦香草的味道，却没有盖住它们的风味。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="12-146">
<summary><h4 id="word-12-146" class="vocabulary-word-title">spicy<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-12-146" aria-label="定位 spicy">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="12-146" data-mark-label="spicy" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="spicy：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="spicy：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=spicy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 spicy" title="在有道查询 spicy">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">12-146</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj.辣的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=spicy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>spicy</strong></a> noodles were so delicious that the children called them yummy and asked for another tasty bowl.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">辣面条太美味，孩子们直说好吃，还想再来一碗可口的面。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Her review called the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=spicy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>spicy</strong></a> curry delicious, while the children simply said it was yummy and tasty.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她的评价称这道辣咖喱非常美味，而孩子们只说它好吃又可口。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">For a formal review of this <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=spicy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>spicy</strong></a> dish, use delicious or tasty; yummy sounds more informal.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">正式评价这道辣菜时可用 delicious 或 tasty；yummy 更口语化。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">spicy food</span> · 辛辣食物</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=savoury&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">savoury</a></span> · 咸鲜的</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mouth-watering&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">mouth-watering</a></span> · 令人垂涎的</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="12-147">
<summary><h4 id="word-12-147" class="vocabulary-word-title">delicious<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-12-147" aria-label="定位 delicious">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="12-147" data-mark-label="delicious" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="delicious：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="delicious：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=delicious&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 delicious" title="在有道查询 delicious">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">12-147</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj.美昧的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The spicy noodles were so <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=delicious&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>delicious</strong></a> that the children called them yummy and asked for another tasty bowl.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">辣面条太美味，孩子们直说好吃，还想再来一碗可口的面。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Her review called the spicy curry <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=delicious&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>delicious</strong></a>, while the children simply said it was yummy and tasty.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她的评价称这道辣咖喱非常美味，而孩子们只说它好吃又可口。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">For a formal review of this spicy dish, use <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=delicious&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>delicious</strong></a> or tasty; yummy sounds more informal.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">正式评价这道辣菜时可用 delicious 或 tasty；yummy 更口语化。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a delicious meal</span> · 美味的一餐</p>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="12-148">
<summary><h4 id="word-12-148" class="vocabulary-word-title">yummy<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-12-148" aria-label="定位 yummy">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="12-148" data-mark-label="yummy" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="yummy：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="yummy：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=yummy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 yummy" title="在有道查询 yummy">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">12-148</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj.美昧的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The spicy noodles were so delicious that the children called them <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=yummy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>yummy</strong></a> and asked for another tasty bowl.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">辣面条太美味，孩子们直说好吃，还想再来一碗可口的面。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Her review called the spicy curry delicious, while the children simply said it was <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=yummy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>yummy</strong></a> and tasty.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她的评价称这道辣咖喱非常美味，而孩子们只说它好吃又可口。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">For a formal review of this spicy dish, use delicious or tasty; <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=yummy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>yummy</strong></a> sounds more informal.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">正式评价这道辣菜时可用 delicious 或 tasty；yummy 更口语化。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">yummy cake</span> · 好吃的蛋糕</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=savoury&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">savoury</a></span> · 咸鲜的</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mouth-watering&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">mouth-watering</a></span> · 令人垂涎的</li></ul>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Her review called the spicy curry delicious, while the children simply said it was yummy and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tasty&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tasty</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她的评价称这道辣咖喱非常美味，而孩子们只说它好吃又可口。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">For a formal review of this spicy dish, use delicious or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tasty&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tasty</strong></a>; yummy sounds more informal.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">正式评价这道辣菜时可用 delicious 或 tasty；yummy 更口语化。</p></li>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=perfume&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>perfume</strong></a> of roses drifted through the open window.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">玫瑰的芳香从敞开的窗户飘进来。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Strong <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=perfume&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>perfume</strong></a> can interfere with a professional food tasting.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">浓烈香水会干扰专业食品品鉴。</p></li>
</ol>

### 词群 12.12｜从饥渴到进食消化 {#group-12-12}

**串联方式：** 由饥饿口渴进入烹饪，再描述吸、吞、舔、嚼与消化。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="12-150">
<summary><h4 id="word-12-150" class="vocabulary-word-title">hunger<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-12-150" aria-label="定位 hunger">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="12-150" data-mark-label="hunger" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="hunger：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="hunger：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hunger&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 hunger" title="在有道查询 hunger">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">12-150</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.饥饿；饥饿感；渴望</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After the long walk, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hunger&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hunger</strong></a> drove us to the café, where we realised how thirsty we were.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">长途步行后，饥饿促使我们进了咖啡馆，我们这才意识到自己有多渴。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Carry water if you get thirsty and a snack to relieve <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hunger&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hunger</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">带水以备口渴时喝，再带点零食充饥。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The relief team gave water to thirsty families and food to those suffering from <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hunger&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hunger</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">救援队给口渴的家庭送水，给忍饥的人们送食物。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">relieve hunger</span> · 缓解饥饿</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dehydration&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">dehydration</a></span> · 脱水</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=satiety&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">satiety</a></span> · 饱足感</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="12-151">
<summary><h4 id="word-12-151" class="vocabulary-word-title">thirsty<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-12-151" aria-label="定位 thirsty">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="12-151" data-mark-label="thirsty" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="thirsty：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="thirsty：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=thirsty&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 thirsty" title="在有道查询 thirsty">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">12-151</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj.渴的；渴望的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After the long walk, hunger drove us to the café, where we realised how <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=thirsty&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>thirsty</strong></a> we were.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">长途步行后，饥饿促使我们进了咖啡馆，我们这才意识到自己有多渴。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Carry water if you get <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=thirsty&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>thirsty</strong></a> and a snack to relieve hunger.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">带水以备口渴时喝，再带点零食充饥。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The relief team gave water to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=thirsty&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>thirsty</strong></a> families and food to those suffering from hunger.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">救援队给口渴的家庭送水，给忍饥的人们送食物。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">feel thirsty</span> · 感到口渴</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dehydration&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">dehydration</a></span> · 脱水</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=satiety&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">satiety</a></span> · 饱足感</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="12-157">
<summary><h4 id="word-12-157" class="vocabulary-word-title">digest<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-12-157" aria-label="定位 digest">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="12-157" data-mark-label="digest" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="digest：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="digest：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=digest&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 digest" title="在有道查询 digest">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">12-157</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.消化；理解，领悟</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Chew food well before you swallow it to make it easier to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=digest&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>digest</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">咽下食物前充分咀嚼，使其更容易消化。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">People who cannot chew or swallow normally may need food that is easy to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=digest&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>digest</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">无法正常咀嚼或吞咽的人可能需要容易消化的食物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Take time to chew each bite rather than swallow quickly and force your stomach to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=digest&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>digest</strong></a> large pieces.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">慢慢咀嚼每一口，不要匆忙吞咽，让胃去消化大块食物。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">digest food</span> · 消化食物</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=digestion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">digestion</a></span> · 消化</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=saliva&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">saliva</a></span> · 唾液</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="12-158">
<summary><h4 id="word-12-158" class="vocabulary-word-title">cook<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-12-158" aria-label="定位 cook">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="12-158" data-mark-label="cook" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="cook：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="cook：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cook&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 cook" title="在有道查询 cook">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">12-158</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.烹调，烧，煮；n.厨师</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">I can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cook&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cook</strong></a> dinner while you bake the bread and fry the onions.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">你烤面包、煎洋葱时，我可以做晚餐。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The course teaches beginners to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cook&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cook</strong></a> soup, bake bread and fry fish.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这门课教初学者煮汤、烤面包和煎鱼。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cook&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cook</strong></a> with less oil, bake the potatoes instead of choosing to fry them.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">想少油烹调，可以烤土豆，而不选择油煎。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">cook a meal</span> · 做一顿饭</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=boil&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">boil</a></span> · 煮沸</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=steam&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">steam</a></span> · 蒸</li></ul>
</div>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="12-159">

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="12-163">
<summary><h4 id="word-12-163" class="vocabulary-word-title">suck<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-12-163" aria-label="定位 suck">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="12-163" data-mark-label="suck" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="suck：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="suck：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=suck&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 suck" title="在有道查询 suck">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">12-163</span></span></summary>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The child wanted to lick an ice lolly, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=suck&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>suck</strong></a> a sweet or chew gum during the journey.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">旅途中，孩子想舔冰棍、吮糖果或嚼口香糖。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">You cannot <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=suck&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>suck</strong></a> a sweet, lick an ice cream and chew gum all at once.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">你不能同时吮糖果、舔冰淇淋和嚼口香糖。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The dentist asked whether she liked to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=suck&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>suck</strong></a> sweets, lick sugary ice lollies or chew sweetened gum.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">牙医问她是否喜欢吮糖果、舔含糖冰棍或嚼加糖口香糖。</p></li>
</ol>

</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="12-164">
<summary><h4 id="word-12-164" class="vocabulary-word-title">swallow<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-12-164" aria-label="定位 swallow">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="12-164" data-mark-label="swallow" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="swallow：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="swallow：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=swallow&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 swallow" title="在有道查询 swallow">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">12-164</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.咽下，吞下</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Chew food well before you <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=swallow&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>swallow</strong></a> it to make it easier to digest.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">咽下食物前充分咀嚼，使其更容易消化。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">People who cannot chew or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=swallow&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>swallow</strong></a> normally may need food that is easy to digest.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">无法正常咀嚼或吞咽的人可能需要容易消化的食物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Take time to chew each bite rather than <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=swallow&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>swallow</strong></a> quickly and force your stomach to digest large pieces.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">慢慢咀嚼每一口，不要匆忙吞咽，让胃去消化大块食物。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">swallow a mouthful</span> · 咽下一口食物</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=digestion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">digestion</a></span> · 消化</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=saliva&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">saliva</a></span> · 唾液</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.咀嚼</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=chew&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Chew</strong></a> food well before you swallow it to make it easier to digest.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">咽下食物前充分咀嚼，使其更容易消化。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">People who cannot <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=chew&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>chew</strong></a> or swallow normally may need food that is easy to digest.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">无法正常咀嚼或吞咽的人可能需要容易消化的食物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Take time to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=chew&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>chew</strong></a> each bite rather than swallow quickly and force your stomach to digest large pieces.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">慢慢咀嚼每一口，不要匆忙吞咽，让胃去消化大块食物。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">chew thoroughly</span> · 充分咀嚼</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=digestion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">digestion</a></span> · 消化</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=saliva&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">saliva</a></span> · 唾液</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.口香糖；树胶</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The child wanted to lick an ice lolly, suck a sweet or chew <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gum&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>gum</strong></a> during the journey.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">旅途中，孩子想舔冰棍、吮糖果或嚼口香糖。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">You cannot suck a sweet, lick an ice cream and chew <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gum&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>gum</strong></a> all at once.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">你不能同时吮糖果、舔冰淇淋和嚼口香糖。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The dentist asked whether she liked to suck sweets, lick sugary ice lollies or chew sweetened <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gum&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>gum</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">牙医问她是否喜欢吮糖果、舔含糖冰棍或嚼加糖口香糖。</p></li>
</ol>

</div>

### 词群 12.13｜备料时的手部动作 {#group-12-13}

**串联方式：** 按浸泡、蘸、挤、搅、磨和切片重建一道菜的准备过程。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="12-168">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=soak&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Soak</strong></a> the beans first, squeeze lemon over the salad, and dip bread into the finished soup.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">先泡豆子，在沙拉上挤柠檬汁，然后用面包蘸做好的汤。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">You can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=soak&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>soak</strong></a> dried fruit in juice, squeeze out the excess liquid, and dip each piece in chocolate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">你可以把干果泡在果汁里，挤掉多余液体，再逐块蘸上巧克力。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=soak&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Soak</strong></a> the cloth, squeeze it gently, then dip one corner into the cleaning solution.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">浸湿抹布，轻轻拧挤，再把一个角蘸入清洁液。</p></li>
</ol>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">While you <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stir&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>stir</strong></a> the soup, I will grind some spices and slice the bread.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">你搅汤的时候，我来磨香料、切面包。</p></li>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Grind the pepper, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=slice&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>slice</strong></a> the mushrooms and stir them into the sauce.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">磨好胡椒，将蘑菇切片，再搅进酱汁中。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">While you stir the soup, I will grind some spices and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=slice&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>slice</strong></a> the bread.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">你搅汤的时候，我来磨香料、切面包。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The recipe tells you when to grind the seeds, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=slice&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>slice</strong></a> the onions and stir the mixture.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">食谱会说明何时磨籽、切洋葱和搅拌混合物。</p></li>

### 本章 Language Takeaway

优先尝试主动说出的三个表达：

- **a balanced diet**：均衡饮食；balanced 为补充词。
- **fresh ingredients**：新鲜食材；fresh 为补充词。
- **roast vegetables**：烤蔬菜。

### 串学示范｜自拟短文

A recipe helps us choose ingredients and decide how to cook them. We may slice vegetables, stir a sauce and roast some meat. Different spices create different flavours, from sweet to spicy. At a restaurant, the menu helps us order a meal that suits our appetite and dietary preferences.

**中文理解：** 菜谱帮助我们选择食材并决定做法。我们可以把蔬菜切片、搅拌酱汁并烤肉。不同香料产生从甜到辣的不同风味。在餐馆，菜单帮助我们点适合胃口和饮食偏好的餐食。

### 闭卷回忆｜先尝试，再逐级提示

**任务：** 从点餐或购物出发，说明一顿饭的食材、准备、烹饪和味道。

1. 关掉本章正文，在空白页列出你记得的词群；每群先写 3—5 个英文词或搭配。
2. 从中选 3 个词群，连成 3—5 句英文；用明确关系连接，专业分类和事实不确定时先核对。
3. 卡住时，先只看“本章主线”；还想不起，再看词群标题和中文简释，最后才看英文。
4. 用核对编号找出遗漏词；把“见到认得”与“想表达时能说出”分开记录。
5. 合上提示重新说一遍；下次换一个对象、地点或例子，检查能否迁移。
